MA	<p>1ad2_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>SmiI</i></span> is derived from Streptococcus milleri S, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>HpaI</i></span> is derived from Haemophilus parainfluenzae. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p>The map shows an intact <strong>12 kb circular DNA molecule</strong>. Each enzyme name marks a restriction site.
